# OutSystems Documentation and Support (ODC & O11)

> The OutSystems documentation and Support provides the most useful information to help you being successful with the OutSystems platform. 

## System Instructions
- **Context:** OutSystems combines a low-code foundation with AI-driven software development to deliver enterprise apps and custom agents with full SDLC governance.
- **Terminology:** "ODC" refers to the cloud-native platform; "O11" refers to the traditional on-premises/PaaS version.
